 The Julio-Claudian emperors felt their ancestry was truly divine. Name one of the two Roman gods to whom they traced their lineage. ANSWER: VENUS / MARS BONUS: Romulus and Remus had a long family tradition of warring brothers. Name their battling grandfather and great-uncle, the latter of whom overthrew the former and removed him from the kingdom. ANSWER: NUMITOR, AMULIUS (accept in either order)
 Which meal would the Romans have called prandium? ANSWER: LUNCH 	BONUS: What was the Roman term for breakfast? ANSWER: IENTACULUM
 Although he probably never chopped down a cherry tree, which early Roman hero is often compared to the father of our country for his restraint in stepping away from power after he served a brief dictatorship in 458 BC? ANSWER: CINCINNATUS BONUS: Which hero of the siege of Veii, four-time triumphator, and five-time dictator was given the honorific title “Second Founder of Rome”? ANSWER: CAMILLUS

ANSWER: OX / HORSE / WOLF / BOAR BONUS: Marius’s military accomplishments included victories over the Germanic Cimbri tribe as well as which north African king? ANSWER: JUGURTHA

ANSWER: NERO BONUS: Which woman, the mother of Nero, was implicated in her husband Claudius’s death and was later murdered at the behest of her son? ANSWER: AGRIPPINA THE YOUNGER

ANSWER: (PRETENDED TO TRIP AND) KISSED THE EARTH

ANSWER:YESTERDAY BONUS: If a third friend, also Roman, tells you multam pecūniam cotīdiē teneō, when do they have money, if our supposedly wealthy friend is telling the truth? ANSWER:EVERY DAY

At what battle did the confines of a mountain pass allow Gavius Pontius to trap the Romans, who were shamefully sent under the yoke by the jubilant Samnites? ANSWER:(BATTLE OF) CAUDINE FORKS BONUS: In what year B.C. did the Battle of Caudine Forks occur? ANSWER:321 (B.C.) BONUS: At what disaster six years later were the Romans under Fabius Maximus Rullianus crushed by the Samnites? ANSWER:(BATTLE OF) LAUTULAE 
Since rule of the Empire’s two halves posthumously passed to his two sons — Arcadius and Honorius — what Christian emperor was the last to rule over a united whole? ANSWER:THEODOSIUS {I // THE GREAT} [PROMPT ON “THEODOSIUS”] BONUS: On one occasion, Theodosius was forced to repent for a massacre by Ambrose, the bishop of what city, from which Constantine had issued an edict that promised to improve treatment of Christians? ANSWER:MILAN / MEDIOLANUM BONUS: At what battle in 394 A.D. did Theodosius defeat the two usurpers Eugenius and Arbogast? ANSWER:FRIGIDUS (RIVER) // FRIGID (RIVER) 
The only male member of the “Capitoline Triad,” worshiped under the name Ēlicius, and revered as Optimus Maximus, what god headed the pantheon of deities in early Rome? ANSWER:JUPITER / JOVE / IUPPITER BONUS: What king of Rome was killed after improperly performing sacrifices to Jupiter Elicius? ANSWER:TULLUS (HOSTILIUS) BONUS: Romulus vowed a temple to Jupiter Feretrius after defeating what king of Caenina in single combat? ANSWER:ACRON (OF CAENINA) 
Command against what foreign ruler was transferred by the Lēx Mānīlia from Lucullus to Pompey, who was able to finally take control of his kingdom of Pontus? ANSWER:MITHRIDATES (VI // THE GREAT // EUPATOR [DIONYSIUS] // OF PONTUS) BONUS: How many wars did the Romans fight against Mithridates? ANSWER:THREE BONUS: The Lēx Mānīlia came in the wake of Mithridates’s victory over a subordinate of Lucullus at what site, where Mithridates’s son Pharnaces II would later be defeated in a proverbially fast campaign? ANSWER:ZELA 
During what war did the Romans develop a boarding technique that relied on devices called corvī, whose use at battles like Mylae allowed them to defeat the Carthaginians for the first time? ANSWER:FIRST PUNIC (WAR) [PROMPT ON “PUNIC WAR”] BONUS: The First Punic War began with a revolt by the Mamertines in a city on what island? ANSWER:SICILY BONUS: What admiral led Rome’s victorious forces at Mylae, winning the city’s first naval battle? ANSWER:(GAIUS) DUILIUS 
What emperor from the Coccēius family was elevated by the senators at an advanced age to serve as successor to Domitian, establishing a precedent of succession for the other four “good emperors”? ANSWER:(MARCUS COCCEIUS) NERVA BONUS: In what year A.D. did Domitian die and Nerva begin his reign? ANSWER:96 (A.D.) BONUS: Due to his unpopularity, Domitian was stricken from the public record and depictions. What two-word Latin phrase meaning “condemnation of memory” do historians use to refer to this erasure? ANSWER:DAMNĀTIŌ MEMORIAE 
What type of event was begun by fetiālēs hurling a spear into the Temple of Bellona before the legiōnēs were directed by the consuls? ANSWER:WAR BONUS: Before consuls, which Roman officials assumed the military responsibilities of the kings, and were attended by 6 lictors while leading an army? ANSWER:PRAETOR BONUS: Name any of the subunits of a Roman legion. ANSWER:COHORT(S), MANIPLE(S), or {CENTURY / CENTURIES} 
What people — who sacked Rome for the first time in 800 years when they attacked in 410 A.D. under the command of Alaric — were divided into two groups that included a Western “Visi-” branch? ANSWER:(VISI)GOTH(S) BONUS: What other Germanic people sacked Rome in 455 A.D. after stealing control of North Africa? ANSWER:VANDAL(S) BONUS: What Vandalic king led their sack of Rome in 455 A.D.? ANSWER:GAISERIC / GENSERIC
